<details>
<summary>Changelog</summary>
<p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/blob/main/packages/effect/CHANGELOG.md">effect's changelog</a>.</em></p>
<blockquote>
<h2>4.0.0-rc.117</h2>
<h3>Patch Changes</h3>
<ul>
<li>
<p><a href="https://redirect.github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8331">#8331</a> <a href="https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/9953c92b860061ec928098b121279087ee56e5e9"><code>9953c92</code></a> Thanks <a href="https://github.com/tim-smart"><code>@​tim-smart</code></a>! - Bound local sends that wait for an unregistered cluster entity type. These sends now fail with an <code>Entity type ... not registered</code> defect at the shared runner registration deadline. Once that startup deadline has elapsed, sends to not-yet-registered dynamic entity types fail immediately.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://redirect.github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8319">#8319</a> <a href="https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/c6e8b20c27b08e94549a9431c38e867aa014b724"><code>c6e8b20</code></a> Thanks <a href="https://github.com/fubhy"><code>@​fubhy</code></a>! - Extend <code>NetAddress.toCanonical</code> to accept internet addresses. IPv4-mapped IPv6 addresses become IPv4 while retaining the port. Other addresses retain their identity and IPv6 scope metadata.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://redirect.github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8317">#8317</a> <a href="https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/8d40572de3762d51b142cfff30e6927ef21e37a3"><code>8d40572</code></a> Thanks <a href="https://github.com/tim-smart"><code>@​tim-smart</code></a>! - Fix cluster workflows stalling after request resets.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://redirect.github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8323">#8323</a> <a href="https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/1b21e0df6ab777d6a1664dfabdea88511f2d25d1"><code>1b21e0d</code></a> Thanks <a href="https://github.com/fubhy"><code>@​fubhy</code></a>! - Add validated multicast address refinements for IP and MAC addresses.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://redirect.github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8310">#8310</a> <a href="https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/3b155e3e24e42b603d48dff5d3280715944998f0"><code>3b155e3</code></a> Thanks <a href="https://github.com/williamrobertson13"><code>@​williamrobertson13</code></a>! - Fix memory leaks in <code>Pool.makeWithTTL</code> with the usage strategy by releasing retired resources and consumed acquisition errors from the TTL queue.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://redirect.github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8326">#8326</a> <a href="https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/895d94410f90c45546aec593730e5630f200ca1b"><code>895d944</code></a> Thanks <a href="https://github.com/lloydrichards"><code>@​lloydrichards</code></a>! - Normalize identified MCP output schemas to object roots across protocol versions.
Support identified object schemas in MCP elicitation requests.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://redirect.github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8318">#8318</a> <a href="https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/705fb6852ac24791352bd78889d57f07cbe949a9"><code>705fb68</code></a> Thanks <a href="https://github.com/fubhy"><code>@​fubhy</code></a>! - Add <code>Queue.shutdownUnsafe</code> to synchronously discard buffered messages and settle pending queue operations from callbacks. Both <code>Queue.shutdown</code> and <code>Queue.shutdownUnsafe</code> return <code>false</code> when the queue has already been shut down or completed; previously, <code>Queue.shutdown</code> always returned <code>true</code>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://redirect.github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8325">#8325</a> <a href="https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/87912c014132648694015a5636177b2ba2bdeb2a"><code>87912c0</code></a> Thanks <a href="https://github.com/fubhy"><code>@​fubhy</code></a>! - Add <code>NetAddress.formatNativeHost</code> and <code>NetAddress.formatMulticastInterface</code> with explicit platform and scope ID map parameters for native socket APIs.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://redirect.github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8308">#8308</a> <a href="https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/106af64cf7117dfad53d3b11b61bb3d17d63bfca"><code>106af64</code></a> Thanks <a href="https://github.com/tristanz"><code>@​tristanz</code></a>! - Release savepoints after nested transactions succeed or successfully roll back in PostgreSQL, PGlite, MySQL, libSQL, and the Node, Bun, React Native, and WASM SQLite clients. This frees PostgreSQL transaction locks before the outer transaction completes.</p>
<p>Custom SQL clients can opt in through the new <code>releaseSavepoint</code> option. Clients that omit it are unchanged.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://redirect.github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8323">#8323</a> <a href="https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/1b21e0df6ab777d6a1664dfabdea88511f2d25d1"><code>1b21e0d</code></a> Thanks <a href="https://github.com/fubhy"><code>@​fubhy</code></a>! - Add generic branded NetAddress classifications and named validating schemas.
Singleton address constants retain their base-family types; guards expose
classifications on demand. Operations that derive new address bits return only
the IPv4 or IPv6 family until callers revalidate the classification.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://redirect.github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8305">#8305</a> <a href="https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/3d59ae6d5f9ff3e52cb6ed4a9f325320580218d5"><code>3d59ae6</code></a> Thanks <a href="https://github.com/tim-smart"><code>@​tim-smart</code></a>! - Make <code>criteria</code> optional for <code>Decision.probability</code>. When supplied, <code>criteria</code> still requires descriptions for both <code>false</code> and <code>true</code>.</p>
<p><code>Decision.Probability.criteria</code> is now optional, so consumers reading outcome descriptions (for example, <code>decision.criteria.true</code>) must first guard against <code>undefined</code>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://redirect.github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8329">#8329</a> <a href="https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/89c9a398412f1f5e7f9c6f697a1badd982bcd741"><code>89c9a39</code></a> Thanks <a href="https://github.com/tim-smart"><code>@​tim-smart</code></a>! - Warn when conflicting cluster workflow definitions reuse a tag.</p>
</li>
</ul>

</details>49:T1279,The Android Device E2E lane reaches Playwright native selectors without installing Playwright's Android driver APKs. On a clean host, `androidDevice.wait` rejects with the instruction to install the Android driver instead of observing the native modal; treating that setup error as an absent selector would hide a real failure.

Install the workspace-pinned Playwright Android assets immediately after the frozen workspace install and before the emulator bundle starts. `--no-install` keeps CLI resolution within the installed workspace. The native-modal catch and downstream accessibility, pairing, and home assertions stay intact.

The production emit regression compiles complete workspace graphs in memory. Its 30-second case budget rejected completed agent emission after 64 seconds in the full suite and 53 seconds in an independently configured rerun. Allow 90 seconds for that full-workspace case; every emit diagnostic and output-path assertion still executes, and the isolated runner retains its 120-second process cap.

Refs #30844, #32942.

Validation at 853cd8454156b93481addcf231870fb46d642866:
- A leased local emulator probe using actual Playwright 1.63.0 reproduced the missing-driver error without the assets. After installing Android assets, the same native-selector probe reached its expected TimeoutError. The probe did not reset or drive the Eliza app.
- Frozen workspace installation and the exact Android asset command passed. Focused Biome and diff checks passed. The initial full tooling suite and configured emit rerun failed at the original 30-second budget; those logs are retained. The revised owning file passed 18 cases/47 assertions (59.62 seconds in the focused run; 93.42 seconds in the complete tooling replay). The complete tooling replay, including its Node lane, and root verification passed at this exact head. All four PR hosted checks passed in run 36853515358. The earlier complete replay exceeded the unchanged 120-second process cap under local load; that failure is retained alongside the successful complete replay.
- Hosted bcabedb Device E2E run 36845567934 failed at the first optional native wait; its privacy-safe report records the location but no raw error. This setup defect is independently reproduced, while the complete hosted onboarding journey remains unverified.

4c:Tea0,# Relates to

[CI 36845574652](https://github.com/elizaOS/eliza/actions/runs/36845574652) ended both Quality attempts with exit 143 during verification. Later, [CI 36851491795](https://github.com/elizaOS/eliza/actions/runs/36851491795) passed Quality but lost two running OS VM qualifications when a second manual CI dispatch at the same commit started. The reusable OS workflow still grouped both manual runs by branch.

# Change

Serialize Quality verification tasks: root `verify` gives each Node task an 8 GB heap, overriding the job's 4 GB setting, so two tasks can exhaust a 16 GB runner. Retain all checks and the job timeout.

Give manually dispatched OS verification runs independent run-ID concurrency groups, matching the CI caller. Only push runs cancel an in-progress run. Independent manual dispatches can now complete their OS qualification instead of cancelling one another.

# Testing

- `actionlint` for both workflows and `git diff --check` passed.
- Windows command coverage, workflow trigger policy and Bun version contracts passed with Bun 1.4.2 / Node 24.15.0.
- A subprocess check confirmed the Quality concurrency override replaces 8 with 1 while retaining typecheck and lint tasks.

<details>
<summary>Changelog</summary>
<p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/github/codeql-action/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md">github/codeql-action/init's changelog</a>.</em></p>
<blockquote>
<h1>CodeQL Action Changelog</h1>
<p>See the <a href="https://github.com/github/codeql-action/releases">releases page</a> for the relevant changes to the CodeQL CLI and language packs.</p>
<h2>[UNRELEASED]</h2>
<p>No user facing changes.</p>
<h2>4.38.2 - 24 Sept 2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>Update default CodeQL bundle version to <a href="https://github.com/github/codeql-action/releases/tag/codeql-bundle-v2.27.1">2.27.1</a>. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/github/codeql-action/pull/4160">#4160</a></li>
</ul>
<h2>4.38.1 - 18 Sept 2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>The CodeQL Action now has experimental support for CodeQL releases for which per-language bundles are available. Per-language bundles support analysis for a single language and are therefore smaller than the combined bundles that allow analysis for all supported languages. As a result, per-language bundles take up less space on disk and are faster to download. We expect to roll this change out to everyone in the coming weeks. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/github/codeql-action/pull/4146">#4146</a></li>
</ul>
<h2>4.38.0 - 09 Sept 2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>On GitHub-hosted runners, the CodeQL Action now deletes unused CodeQL bundles from the toolcache before downloading a different bundle, which frees up disk space for the analysis. We expect to roll this change out to everyone in September. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/github/codeql-action/pull/4124">#4124</a></li>
<li>The CodeQL Action now supports CodeQL releases that are compatible with Linux Arm64 and downloads the native <code>linux-arm64</code> CodeQL bundle when available. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/github/codeql-action/pull/4072">#4072</a></li>
<li>Update default CodeQL bundle version to <a href="https://github.com/github/codeql-action/releases/tag/codeql-bundle-v2.27.0">2.27.0</a>. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/github/codeql-action/pull/4129">#4129</a></li>
</ul>
<h2>4.37.9 - 26 Aug 2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>Update default CodeQL bundle version to <a href="https://github.com/github/codeql-action/releases/tag/codeql-bundle-v2.26.4">2.26.4</a>. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/github/codeql-action/pull/4106">#4106</a></li>
</ul>
<h2>4.37.8 - 21 Aug 2026</h2>
<p>No user facing changes.</p>
<h2>4.37.7 - 13 Aug 2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>Update default CodeQL bundle version to <a href="https://github.com/github/codeql-action/releases/tag/codeql-bundle-v2.26.3">2.26.3</a>. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/github/codeql-action/pull/4085">#4085</a></li>
</ul>
<h2>4.37.6 - 04 Aug 2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>Changed the default filepath for the new remote file address format that was introduced in CodeQL Action 4.37.0 / 3.37.0 to <code>.github/codeql-config.yml</code> to align it with the suggested path that is used elsewhere. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/github/codeql-action/pull/4070">#4070</a></li>
</ul>
<h2>4.37.5 - 03 Aug 2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>Fixed a bug where a network error while streaming the download of the CodeQL bundle could terminate the <code>init</code> Action instead of falling back to downloading the bundle before extracting it. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/github/codeql-action/pull/4061">#4061</a></li>
</ul>
<h2>4.37.4 - 29 Jul 2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>This version of the CodeQL Action adds support for the <code>tools</code> input for the <code>codeql-action/init</code> step to be specified using a <code>github-codeql-tools</code> <a href="https://docs.github.com/en/organizations/managing-organization-settings/managing-custom-properties-for-repositories-in-your-organization">repository property</a>. This feature will gradually be rolled out following the release of this version. Once rolled out, this allows for the CodeQL CLI version that is used in GitHub-managed workflows, such as Default Setup, to be set to a custom value. For example, customers who run into issues with rate limits when a new CodeQL CLI version is released can set the value to <code>toolcache</code> to always use the CodeQL CLI version that is available in the runner toolcache. For Advanced Setup workflows, the value provided for <code>tools</code> in the workflow definition always takes precedence unless the value of the repository property starts with <code>!</code>. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/github/codeql-action/pull/4037">#4037</a></li>
<li>Update default CodeQL bundle version to <a href="https://github.com/github/codeql-action/releases/tag/codeql-bundle-v2.26.2">2.26.2</a>. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/github/codeql-action/pull/4051">#4051</a></li>
</ul>
<h2>4.37.3 - 22 Jul 2026</h2>
<p>No user facing changes.</p>
<!-- raw HTML omitted -->
</blockquote>

Updates `github/codeql-action/analyze` from 4.38.1 to 4.38.2
<details>
<summary>Release notes</summary>
<p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/github/codeql-action/releases">github/codeql-action/analyze's releases</a>.</em></p>
<blockquote>
<h2>v4.38.2</h2>
<ul>
<li>Update default CodeQL bundle version to <a href="https://github.com/github/codeql-action/releases/tag/codeql-bundle-v2.27.1">2.27.1</a>. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/github/codeql-action/pull/4160">#4160</a></li>
</ul>
</blockquote>
</details>
<details>
<summary>Changelog</summary>
<p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/github/codeql-action/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md">github/codeql-action/analyze's changelog</a>.</em></p>
<blockquote>
<h1>CodeQL Action Changelog</h1>
<p>See the <a href="https://github.com/github/codeql-action/releases">releases page</a> for the relevant changes to the CodeQL CLI and language packs.</p>
<h2>[UNRELEASED]</h2>
<p>No user facing changes.</p>
<h2>4.38.2 - 24 Sept 2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>Update default CodeQL bundle version to <a href="https://github.com/github/codeql-action/releases/tag/codeql-bundle-v2.27.1">2.27.1</a>. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/github/codeql-action/pull/4160">#4160</a></li>
</ul>
<h2>4.38.1 - 18 Sept 2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>The CodeQL Action now has experimental support for CodeQL releases for which per-language bundles are available. Per-language bundles support analysis for a single language and are therefore smaller than the combined bundles that allow analysis for all supported languages. As a result, per-language bundles take up less space on disk and are faster to download. We expect to roll this change out to everyone in the coming weeks. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/github/codeql-action/pull/4146">#4146</a></li>
</ul>
<h2>4.38.0 - 09 Sept 2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>On GitHub-hosted runners, the CodeQL Action now deletes unused CodeQL bundles from the toolcache before downloading a different bundle, which frees up disk space for the analysis. We expect to roll this change out to everyone in September. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/github/codeql-action/pull/4124">#4124</a></li>
<li>The CodeQL Action now supports CodeQL releases that are compatible with Linux Arm64 and downloads the native <code>linux-arm64</code> CodeQL bundle when available. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/github/codeql-action/pull/4072">#4072</a></li>
<li>Update default CodeQL bundle version to <a href="https://github.com/github/codeql-action/releases/tag/codeql-bundle-v2.27.0">2.27.0</a>. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/github/codeql-action/pull/4129">#4129</a></li>
</ul>
<h2>4.37.9 - 26 Aug 2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>Update default CodeQL bundle version to <a href="https://github.com/github/codeql-action/releases/tag/codeql-bundle-v2.26.4">2.26.4</a>. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/github/codeql-action/pull/4106">#4106</a></li>
</ul>
<h2>4.37.8 - 21 Aug 2026</h2>
<p>No user facing changes.</p>
<h2>4.37.7 - 13 Aug 2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>Update default CodeQL bundle version to <a href="https://github.com/github/codeql-action/releases/tag/codeql-bundle-v2.26.3">2.26.3</a>. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/github/codeql-action/pull/4085">#4085</a></li>
</ul>
<h2>4.37.6 - 04 Aug 2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>Changed the default filepath for the new remote file address format that was introduced in CodeQL Action 4.37.0 / 3.37.0 to <code>.github/codeql-config.yml</code> to align it with the suggested path that is used elsewhere. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/github/codeql-action/pull/4070">#4070</a></li>
</ul>
<h2>4.37.5 - 03 Aug 2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>Fixed a bug where a network error while streaming the download of the CodeQL bundle could terminate the <code>init</code> Action instead of falling back to downloading the bundle before extracting it. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/github/codeql-action/pull/4061">#4061</a></li>
</ul>
<h2>4.37.4 - 29 Jul 2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>This version of the CodeQL Action adds support for the <code>tools</code> input for the <code>codeql-action/init</code> step to be specified using a <code>github-codeql-tools</code> <a href="https://docs.github.com/en/organizations/managing-organization-settings/managing-custom-properties-for-repositories-in-your-organization">repository property</a>. This feature will gradually be rolled out following the release of this version. Once rolled out, this allows for the CodeQL CLI version that is used in GitHub-managed workflows, such as Default Setup, to be set to a custom value. For example, customers who run into issues with rate limits when a new CodeQL CLI version is released can set the value to <code>toolcache</code> to always use the CodeQL CLI version that is available in the runner toolcache. For Advanced Setup workflows, the value provided for <code>tools</code> in the workflow definition always takes precedence unless the value of the repository property starts with <code>!</code>. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/github/codeql-action/pull/4037">#4037</a></li>
<li>Update default CodeQL bundle version to <a href="https://github.com/github/codeql-action/releases/tag/codeql-bundle-v2.26.2">2.26.2</a>. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/github/codeql-action/pull/4051">#4051</a></li>
</ul>
<h2>4.37.3 - 22 Jul 2026</h2>
<p>No user facing changes.</p>
<!-- raw HTML omitted -->
</blockquote>

<summary>Dependabot commands and options</summary>
<br />

You can trigger Dependabot actions by commenting on this PR:
- `@dependabot rebase` will rebase this PR
- `@dependabot recreate` will recreate this PR, overwriting any edits that have been made to it
- `@dependabot show <dependency name> ignore conditions` will show all of the ignore conditions of the specified dependency
- `@dependabot ignore <dependency name> major version` will close this group update PR and stop Dependabot creating any more for the specific dependency's major version (unless you unignore this specific dependency's major version or upgrade to it yourself)
- `@dependabot ignore <dependency name> minor version` will close this group update PR and stop Dependabot creating any more for the specific dependency's minor version (unless you unignore this specific dependency's minor version or upgrade to it yourself)
- `@dependabot ignore <dependency name>` will close this group update PR and stop Dependabot creating any more for the specific dependency (unless you unignore this specific dependency or upgrade to it yourself)
- `@dependabot unignore <dependency name>` will remove all of the ignore conditions of the specified dependency
- `@dependabot unignore <dependency name> <ignore condition>` will remove the ignore condition of the specified dependency and ignore conditions

# Change

`packages/cloud/e2e/package.json` still declares `"test:mock-llm": "bun test src/fixtures/mock-llm.test.ts"`, but `55f4f25689` ("remove more slop", 2026-09-23) deleted that suite together with the script line, and the merge `1294b3e12d` later brought the line back without the file. On develop the script fails before running anything:

```
$ cd packages/cloud/e2e && bun test src/fixtures/mock-llm.test.ts
note: Tests need ".test", "_test_", ".spec" or "_spec_" in the filename (ex: "MyApp.test.ts")
note: To treat the "src/fixtures/mock-llm.test.ts" filter as a path, run "bun test ./src/fixtures/mock-llm.test.ts"
exit 1
```

This removes the script. Nothing invokes it: `git grep -n "test:mock-llm"` over the repository (workflows, package guides, scripts) matches only the declaration itself. The remaining fixture `src/fixtures/mock-llm.ts` is unchanged and still used by the Playwright stack.

53:T1b13,# Relates to

Fixes #32959.

`Validate develop` is red at the current tip `4573712ebf`: the
`cloud / agent-stability / deterministic-keyless` lane fails all three attempts
and `Complete manifest` fails with it
(https://github.com/elizaOS/eliza/actions/runs/36636159978). Each attempt's
`scenario.stdout.log` in the `cloud-stability-keyless-1` artifact says:

```text
deterministic model fixtures were not consumed:
[{"name":"cloud-reminder-dispatch-body","consumed":0,"min":1,"max":1,"required":true},
 {"name":"cloud-reminder-dispatch-title","consumed":0,"min":1,"max":1,"required":true}]
```

Since `51bd56787c` (#32931, "deliver grounded one-shot content without prose
rewriting") the `exactReminder` branch in
`plugins/plugin-personal-assistant/src/lifeops/domains/reminders-service.ts`
builds the in-app body with `buildReminderBody` and issues a
`ReminderPresentation` titled `Reminder`, so no `TEXT_SMALL` call happens for
an owner one-shot `OWNER_REMINDERS` reminder. The deterministic scenario still
declared two required `TEXT_SMALL` fixtures for that path.

# Change

Remove the two dead fixtures (`cloud-reminder-dispatch-body`,
`cloud-reminder-dispatch-title`) from
`packages/cloud/e2e/scenarios/cloud-stability-agent.scenario.ts`. The
scenario's `assertCloudReadback` guard still pins the dispatched notification
(`category: "reminder"`, `source: "lifeops"`, body containing
`synthetic Cloud inbox`, `groupKey` prefixed `reminder:`), which the grounded
body `Reminder: Review the synthetic Cloud inbox` satisfies. No runtime code
changes; the real-model lane uses the same scenario without fixtures.

# Testing

- [x] Targets `develop` and has no conflicts with the latest `origin/develop` (branch is `4573712ebf` + one commit).
- [ ] Ran `bun install` and `bun run verify` after syncing; record blockers below.
- [x] Updated relevant documentation. N/A - test scenario only.

- `bunx @biomejs/biome check scenarios/cloud-stability-agent.scenario.ts` (packages/cloud/e2e): `Checked 1 file in 3s. No fixes applied.`
- Hosted proof is being produced by dispatching this repository's own `Cloud Agent Stability` workflow (`cloud-stability.yml`, `real_model=false`) on the contributor fork at this head and, as a control, at the red develop tip `4573712ebf`; run links are added below as they complete.
